# Known exceptions for tools/publish_check.py
#
# Blank lines and lines starting with # are ignored.
# Paths are relative to the repository root and use forward slashes.
#
#   size:<path>             this file may be over 500 KB
#   unreferenced:<path>     this asset may have no HTML href/src or CSS url()
#   missing:<html> <url>    this href or src may point at a missing local file
#   root:<html>             this page may link into preview/ or show the PREVIEW banner
#
# A bare path is exempt from both the size check and the unreferenced check.

# Tells GitHub Pages to serve the files as they are, without Jekyll.
unreferenced:.nojekyll

# Cloudflare Pages redirects from removed report pages and the site map.
unreferenced:_redirects
